# Engine alert histories — three HOUSE organs (COO ruling 38 Part A)

Engine pin: `72082493cb5e29cf5fe4a093fadf6f435b6fe2c3` (primary); legacy `896471652cea` (backfill writer). Prices: **restated** as of capture. Every state is the engine's own output; the engine was imported and called, never edited.

These are engine alert histories only. Nothing in this file relates to outcomes, returns, forward performance or any grading; every state is the engine's own descriptive output replayed on restated prices.

The first file was built in a sparse working tree that lacked seven files the engine reads: the fitted cycle bands, the ladder risk calibration, the overlay gate, the two index membership files behind the amalgam families, and the two hazard model files. The engine ran on its defaults for each of them (the hazard loader logs one warning; the others log nothing), and the build took its price inputs at the engine pin commit rather than the branch head. Its sidecar recorded none of this. A build in a tree lacking the same files reproduces that file byte for byte. The fitted bands are shown to change the output. The risk calibration is all 1.0 at the pin and removing it alone changed nothing. The overlay gate's absent default equals the file's value at the pin. The effect of the membership files and the hazard files has not been measured separately. It was replaced before delivery.

| organ | keys | first knowable | rows | fidelity match/total |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| us_basket | 49 | ≈2024-05 (dated, 252-session floor); current as far back as inputs reach | 587707 | 1803/1911 |
| phase_v2 | 11 | per-key 300-bar floor + MTF warm (nine 2000-03-01, XLC ≈2019-08, XLRE 2016-12-14) | 66618 | backfill 652/1881; forward 601/858 |
| rolling_over | 11 | per-key 300-bar floor + MTF warm (same as phase_v2) | 64737 | 110/110 |

### D-1 forward-log table (current engine; re-derived)

Population = forward-log rows whose (date,id) is a session date present in the replay. D-1 = previous session date for that id in the replay. equal at D = stored == replay(id,D); equal at D-1 = stored == replay(id,D-1). Replay not shifted; no row re-keyed.

Population = 682.

| | equal at D-1 | NOT equal at D-1 | row total |
|---|---|---|---|
| equal at D | 577 (both) | 24 (D only) | 601 |
| NOT equal at D | 34 (D-1 only) | 47 (neither) | 81 |

Restricted to rows differing at D (81 rows): D-1 only = 34, neither = 47.
The live log stamps 34 rows with the benchmark date while that sector's tape lagged a session (live-store behaviour, not repaired by us).
- **rolling_over vs live engine, branch tape** (latest 10 sessions): 110/110 match, 0 mismatch.
